Algorithm idea (Weighted Multiplicative Active-Set):
- Normalize sum=1.
- Compute c, choose active top K lags.
- Compute g_i = 2∑_{s∈K} w_s a_{s-i}.
- For a candidate scale η, set a' = a * exp(-η g_i) (or normalized exp(log a - η g)). Use line search to reduce max c. Nonnegative preserved. This is gradient descent in relative entropy.
- Update active set.
- Periodically random initialized, or cool K from large to small.

Let's write L-BFGS function:
```
def lbfgs_fun_v(x):
    n = LBF_N
    beta = LBF_BETA
    theta = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    # do not modify x; copy
    th = theta.copy()
    # bound maybe clip to LBF_CLIP in case
    th = np.clip(th, -LBF_CLIP, LBF_CLIP)
    th[0] = 0.0
    m = np.max(th)
    e = np.exp(th - m)
    a = e / e.sum()
    c = convolve_self(a, n, LBF_FSIZE) # uses global fsize maybe
    z = beta * (c - c.max())
    # if beta too large? z <=0, safe. sum exp.
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = ez.sum()
    loss = (c.max() + np.log(s)) / beta
    w = ez / s
    grad_a = 2.0 * cross_corr(w, a, n, LBF_FSIZE)
    dot = np.dot(a, grad_a)
    grad_th = a * grad_a - a * dot
    grad_th[0] = 0.0
    return loss, grad_th
```
Need `cross_corr(w,a,n,fsize)`: return array length n: ifft(fft(w,fsize)*conj(fft(a,fsize)))[:n]. But note w length 2n-1 zero padded in fsize. c length 2n-1. `convolve_self` returns c length 2n-1. Good. Need fsize at least len(w)+n-1 = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2? Wait for cross convolution/correlation, to avoid circular wrap, need fsize ≥ len(w)+n-1 = 3n-2. Previous code used fsize = 1 << (len(w)+n-1).bit_length in grad functions. But for convolve self, fsize = 1 << (2n-1 -1).bit_length = next pow2 of 2n-1. That is ~2n. For cross w length 2n-1 and a length n, cyclic convolution length len(w)+n-1 =3n-2. If we use same fsize 2n, circular alias can occur! Did previous code have a bug? In `fft_grad_soft`, they set `fsize = 1 << (len(w) + n - 1).bit_length()`, yes for gradient bigger. In convolve self fsize=2n-1 padded. For L-BFGS we can use one fsize for both: need max(2n-1, len(w)+n-1)=3n-2. Using fsize=3n times next pow2 for n=8192: 3*8192-2=24574, next pow2 32768. Not 16384. Wait conv self only needs 16384. But cross needs 24576? 2n-1=16383, n=8192, sum=24575, next pow2 32768. If use 16384, wrap of w*a? Let's examine. w indices 0..2n-2, a 0..n-1. Linear product correlation h[m]=∑ w[k] a[k-m] for 0≤m<n. This is cross-correlation. Circular length N. Aliasing if terms with k-m+N within 0..n-1. Since k-m can be negative down to -(n-1); mod N = N - (m-k), which could be large >n-1? If N<2n? For N=2n-2 maybe N-(m-k) ranges? Max k=2n-2, min m=0 -> k-m=2n-2 <N? Actually N for conv self 2n-1? If N=2n-1, k-m max 2n-2=N-1; min negative when k<m. Negative min - (n-1) -> N-(n-1)=n? outside a support 0..n-1? zero. So N=2n-1 might suffice? Let's check negative: k=0, m=n-1 => k-m=-n+1, mod 2n-1 = n. a[n]=0 (zero padded length n), okay. Positive terms k-m in [0,n-1] valid when k≥m. So no alias into 0..n-1 from negative because N - q where q≤n-1 -> N-q ≥ n. Good. Need N ≥ 2n-1. So conv self fsize 2n-1 (next pow2) enough for cross with w length 2n-1? In ifft(W*conj(A)), convolution length N; linear terms for h[m] get contributions w[k] a[k-m] modulo. Negative map to indices N-(m-k) which are ≥ N-(n-1). If N=2n-1, ≥n, outside. Positive terms k-m max 2n-2 < N. So okay. Need N ≥2n-1. Thus fsize for gradient same as conv self (next pow2 of 2n-1). Previous used bigger but unnecessary. Good. For n=8192, 2n-1=16383, next pow2=16384. Great. Wait N=16384 exactly 2n? 2n-1=16383, next pow2 16384. N-(n-1)=8193 >=n (8192) zero. Good. If n power of two? 2n-1 not power, N=2n. Good. For n not power, N=next pow2 >2n-1. fine.

Need convert initial direct arrays to theta with first fixed 0:
```
def array_to_theta(a, clip=50.0):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, float), 0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: a = np.ones_like(a); s=1.0
    a = a/s
    # add floor to avoid log0
    eps = np.finfo(np.float64).eps
    a = a + eps
    a = a / a.sum()
    th = np.log(a)
    # fix first = 0, but avoid overflow: subtract first then clip
    th = th - th[0]
    # This may have values below -clip if zero; clip
    th = np.clip(th, -clip, clip)
    # After clipping, first maybe 0; if we clip positive? first 0
    th[0]=0.0
    return th
```

def polish_top(a, n, fsize, max_time):
    a = normalize(a,1)
    best_obj=2*n*maxc; best_a=a.copy(); alpha=1.0/(n?); etc.
    while time<deadline:
        c=convolve_self(a); m=c.max(); obj=2n*m; update.
        k = min(256, max(4, int(16 * min(1, (deadline-now)/...?)?)))
        idx = argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        w = np.zeros(size); w[idx] = 1/k
        grad = 2*cross(w,a)
        gnorm = norm(grad)
        if gnorm small: break
        # candidate multiplicative?
        # Use projected additive with alpha adaptive
        for alpha in [alpha_cur, 0.5*alpha_cur, 2*alpha_cur?]:
            b=project_simplex(a - alpha*grad,1)
            objb=...
            if objb < obj*(1-1e-9): accept, alpha_cur = alpha*1.1; break
        else: alpha_cur*=0.5; maybe if too small break
    return best
```

Let's design `lp_descent(a, n, fsize, max_time, Kini, rng)`:
- Normalize a sum=1; best local.
- active K starts e.g. 32 or 64. For some iterations:
  - c=conv(a); m=c.max(); obj=2n m.
  - active indices = top K of c. To avoid duplicate? Use np.argpartition.
  - Build A matrix K x n with cross coefficients a[s-j]. But constraints are `conv(a,b)_s <= m` (not current M? Yes M=current max). If we rescale b, conv of combined may reduce.
  - Solve LP: maximize ∑ b_j subject to A b ≤ m, 0≤b≤U. Note A rows maybe all zero? Add small positive? no.
  - If solve failed or b sum <=0, break/continue with smaller K.
  - b_norm = b / b.sum()
  - Try mixing: for t in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.2,0.05?], `cand = (1-t)*a + t*b_norm`; normalize; compute obj. Accept largest t that improves (or even equal?). If none, try smaller (0.01) or skip. If accept, update a, recompute active. If improve objective by >1e-10.
  - Update K maybe increase every few if progress: K=min(256, K+8).
  - Stop if time.
- Return best.

Why this descent? Need ensure t line search. We can try t values sorted [0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]. Since b shape may be too far, small t. If b=a, no progress. If objective not improve, b didn't help; maybe need include more constraints. Increase K and rerun.

LP solve details:
```
from scipy.optimize import linprog
c_obj = -np.ones(n)
A = active_matrix(a, n, active_idx)
b_ub = m * np.ones(K)
bounds = [(0.0, U)] * n
res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, ...})
```

- `theta_loss_grad(x)`:
```
def theta_loss_grad(x):
    n=G_N; fsize=G_FSIZE; beta=G_BETA; clip=G_CLIP
    th = np.array(x, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    np.clip(th, -clip, clip, out=th)
    th[0]=0.0
    mx = np.max(th)
    e = np.exp(th-mx)
    a = e / e.sum()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    csize = 2*n-1
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:csize]
    cmax = float(c.max())
    exact = 2.0*n*cmax
    if exact < G_BEST_OBJ and np.isfinite(exact):
         G_BEST_OBJ=exact
         G_BEST_THETA = th.copy()
         G_BEST_N = n
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    # to avoid underflow okay
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = ez.sum()
    if s == 0: loss_base = cmax; w = np.zeros(csize); w[np.argmax(c)] = 1
    else: loss_base = cmax + np.log(s)/beta; w = ez/s
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_a = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    dot = np.dot(a, grad_a)
    grad = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad[0]=0.0
    factor = 2.0*n
    return factor*loss_base, factor*grad
```
Potential bug: `loss_base` minimized, but if β high, loss_base ≈ cmax - logmult/β. Multiplying by 2n. Good.

But if β very high and cmax diff small, z = beta*(c-cmax). If beta=1e6, z for c slightly below max -0.005, exp 0.995; fine. If diff 1e-4, exp(-100)=3e-44. No overflow. If beta*(c-cmax) could be positive due cmax? zero. Good.

```
def run_lbfgs(theta0, n, fsize, beta, maxiter):
    global G_N,...
    G_N=n; G_FSIZE=fsize; G_BETA=beta; G_CLIP=G_CLIP
    x0 = np.asarray(theta0,float).copy()
    if x0[0] !=0: assume we should not mutate? We'll clip then set x0[0]=0.
    np.clip(x0, -G_CLIP, G_CLIP, out=x0)
    x0[0]=0.0
    bounds = [(0.0,0.0)] + [(-G_CLIP,G_CLIP)]*(n-1)
    res = minimize(theta_loss_grad, x0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=dict(maxiter=int(maxiter), ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-9, maxls=10, disp=False))
    return res.x if success else x0
```

def search_for_n(n, max_seconds, rng, parent_arr=None):
    start_n=time.monotonic(); deadline_n=start_n+max_seconds
    fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr)
    # If none, add uniform theta zeros
    if not inits: inits=[np.zeros(n)]
    # Maybe sort inits? parent first
    # Phase 1: low beta 20, maxiter 200-300
    scale_maxit = max(40, int(250 * 8192 / n))
    for idx, th in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline_n - 5: break
       # For parent maybe also run beta 50?
       beta = 20.0 if idx>0 else 50.0
       maxit = scale_maxit
       th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, maxit)
       # quick exact update global? run_lbfgs did. But compute local best:
       obj,a=exact... if obj < local_best: update local
    # Phase 2 top local states: we have list results [(obj, th)]
    results after phase1. Sort.
    top = min(4, len(results))
    beta_stages = [(50, scale_maxit), (150, scale_maxit), (500, scale_maxit)]
    for each top in time: for beta,maxit in stages: if time break; th=run_lbfgs(th,...); exact; if better local update or global
    # Phase 3 random perturbations from local best
    if time remains:
       th_best = local_best_theta
       for sigma in [0.25,0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01]:
          th_noisy = th_best + rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
          # after noise, maybe pick anchor of noisy
          th=run_lbfgs(th_noisy,n,fsize,beta=500,maxit=scale_maxit//2)
          exact... update
    # Phase 4 LP polish
    remaining = deadline_n - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30 and n <= 16384:
       lp_time = min(remaining*0.5, 300?) maybe; but LP solve time unknown.
       a = theta_to_a(local_best_theta,n)
       a,best_obj = lp_descent(a,n,fsize,lp_time, rng, max_K=128)
       exact; update local/global
```

LP descent implementation top-level:
```
def lp_descent(a, n, fsize, max_time, rng, Kinit=32, Kmax=128):
    a = np.clip(a,0,None); a/=a.sum()
    c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=c.max(); best_obj=2*n*m; best_a=a.copy()
    K= Kinit
    fail_count=0
    stall=0
    t_extra = 0.1
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max_time
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2 and K <= Kmax:
       c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=float(c.max()); obj=2*n*m
       if obj < best_obj: best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy()
       # select active top K
       if K > c.size: K=c.size
       idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size-K)[:? Actually want top K. np.argpartition(c, c.shape[0]-K)[-K:]? Simpler:
       part = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K < len(c) else np.arange(len(c))
       # If K=1, -1 works.
       A = build_active_matrix(a, n, part, m)
       # maybe if A row all zeros? continue
       try solve
       if fail: ...
       b = np.maximum(res.x,0)
       s=b.sum()
       if s <= 1e-12: fail
       b=b/s
       # line search t
       accepted=False
       for t in [0.4,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
           cand = (1-t)*a + t*b
           s2=cand.sum(); if s2<=0: continue
           cand/=s2
           c2=convolve_self(cand,n,fsize); obj2=2*n*c2.max()
           if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
               a=cand; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # try using M slightly lower? or increase K
           stall +=1
           # maybe break if high stall
           if stall > 6: break
       else:
           stall=0
       # Update K: if progress, maybe increase slightly; if not, increase more
       if stall == 0:
           K = min(Kmax, K + max(4, K//8))
       else:
           K = min(Kmax, K * 2)
    return best_a, best_obj
```

def build_active_matrix(a,n,active,m):
    K=len(active); A=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    aminv = 1.0/m
    for r,s in enumerate(active):
       row=A[r]; lo=max(0,s-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,s)
       start=s-hi; end=s-lo+1
       # slice decreasing
       row[lo:hi+1] = a[start:end][::-1] * aminv

    return A

Now, let's think about L-BFGS gradient correctness in more detail. Objective L = (1/β) log∑ e^{β c_s}. Derivative ∂L/∂a = (1/β) * 1/Z ∑ e^{β c_s} β * ∂c_s/∂a = ∑ w_s ∂c_s/∂a. ∂c_s/∂a_j = 2 a_{s-j} (if valid). Represented by `cross_corr(w,a)` = ∑_s w_s a_{s-j}. grad_a = 2 cross. Good. The loss is in a, but a = softmax(th). The gradient wrt th is J^T grad_a. For softmax a_i = e_i/Z, ∂a_i/∂th_k = a_i (δ_{ik} - a_k). So gradθ_k = grad_a_k a_k - a_k ∑_i grad_a_i a_i. Yes. We set fixed anchor gradient zero. Since full softmax has gradient sum zero, anchor constraint okay? If we fix anchor 0, θ lives in codimension 1. The gradient projected is same except anchor coordinate zero. Good. But if anchor not variable, do we need adjust dot? The chain for non-anchor coordinates: gradθ_k = a_k(grad_a_k - dot). For anchor, zero. This is gradient for the constrained parameterization. Good.

Let's detail `search_for_n` top-level fully:
```
def search_for_n(n, max_seconds, rng, parent_arr=None):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    fsize = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    local_obj = G_BEST_OBJ if G_BEST_N == n else np.inf
    local_theta = G_BEST_THETA if G_BEST_N == n else None
    # if local theta None, evaluate parent etc.
    inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr)
    results=[]
    if local_theta is not None:
       # include it as init/result? maybe not, local best already.
       results.append((local_obj, local_theta))
    # Maybe if no local and parent result? make_initializations includes parent.
    # Add uniform zeros to be safe
    if not inits:
       inits = [np.zeros(n, dtype=float)]
    # Limit inits to time: maybe 28
    max_inits = 24
    if len(inits)> max_inits: inits=inits[:max_inits]
    scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0/n))
    maxit1 = int(max(30, 120*scale))
    maxit2 = int(max(50, 250*scale))
    maxit3 = int(max(40, 180*scale))
    beta1 = 2000.0
    # Phase 1
    for idx, th0 in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
       # maybe if len==n? ensure
       th0 = np.asarray(th0, float); if th0.size != n: th0 = interpolate?
       th = run_lbfgs(th0, n, fsize, beta1, maxit1)
       obj,a = exact_obj_a_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
       if obj < local_obj:
           local_obj=obj; local_theta=th
       results.append((obj,th))
    # Sort results, remove duplicates? no
    results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # Wait lambda not allowed! Rule no lambda functions. Need avoid lambda. Use `results.sort(key=results_key?)` Can't use lambda. We can define top-level function `first_element(t): return t[0]` and use `results.sort(key=first_element)`. Good.
    # Phase 2 continuation on top results
    top = min(4, len(results))
    for i in range(top):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       th = results[i][1]
       for beta, maxit in [(10000, maxit2), (100000, maxit2), (1000000, maxit3)]:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15: break
          th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, maxit)
          obj,a = exact_obj_a_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
          if obj < local_obj: local_obj=obj; local_theta=th
          # maybe update results? not necessary
    # Phase 3 random restarts from local best
    if local_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
       for sigma in [0.3,0.18,0.1,0.06,0.03,0.015]:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
          th_noisy = local_theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
          # choose anchor of noisy? run_lbfgs will shift anchor from x[anchor]. But we haven't set global anchor; run_lbfgs default anchor maybe max of noisy? We'll pass anchor.
          anchor = int(np.argmax(th_noisy))
          th = run_lbfgs(th_noisy, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit3, anchor=anchor)
          obj,a = exact_obj_a_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
          if obj < local_obj: local_obj=obj; local_theta=th
    # Phase 4 LP polish
    if local_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and n <= 16384:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       lp_time = max(10.0, min(200.0, rem*0.4))
       a = theta_to_a(local_theta, n)
       a, obj_lp = lp_descent(a, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, Kinit=24, Kmax=128 if n<=8192 else 96)
       if obj_lp < local_obj:
          local_obj=obj_lp; local_theta=array_to_theta(a, 60.0)
          # Maybe run high beta L-BFGS from LP result
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
              th2 = run_lbfgs(local_theta, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2)
              obj2,a2=...

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

Need be careful with global assignments in functions: Use `global G_N, G_FSIZE, ...` at top of functions that modify. In `theta_loss_grad`, assign G_BEST_OBJ, etc. So declare global.

Let's inspect `theta_loss_grad` updating global:
```
def theta_loss_grad(x):
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA
    n=G_N; ...
    th = np.array(x, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    np.clip(th, -G_CLIP, G_CLIP, out=th)
    if n>0: th[G_ANCHOR]=0.0
    mx = th.max()
    e = np.exp(th-mx)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): uniform
    a = e / s
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    csize = 2*n-1
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:csize]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    exact = 2.0 * n * cmax
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < G_BEST_OBJ - 1e-14:
        G_BEST_OBJ = exact
        G_BEST_N = n
        G_BEST_A = a.copy()
        G_BEST_THETA = th.copy()
    # logsumexp
    z = G_BETA * (c - cmax)
    # if any z not finite? if c nan? set zeros
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        idx = np.argmax(c); cmax? but c should finite.
    ez = np.exp(z)
    zsum = ez.sum()
    if zsum <= 0: ...
    loss_base = cmax + np.log(zsum) / G_BETA
    w = ez / zsum
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_a = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad[G_ANCHOR]=0.0
    factor = 2.0*n
    return float(factor*loss_base), grad * factor
```
Potential: If G_BETA very high (100000), `z` min maybe -100, exp underflows to 0. zsum maybe 0 if all z underflow? At least cmax term z=0 -> exp1, zsum≥1. Good.
If cmax is negative tiny? z for cmax 0; exact negative? c from nonnegative should ≥ -1e-15; exact maybe negative small? Update global if exact positive; if negative due numerical, objective could be negative impossible. We can `if cmax < 0: cmax=0.0`. Then exact nonnegative. Use `cmax = max(0.0, float(c.max()))`. For z, if cmax set 0 but max c negative? c all negative tiny? Not. Use `cmax_raw = float(c.max()); cmax = cmax_raw if cmax_raw > 0 else 1e-300`; But if all negative due bug, exp okay. Since a positive, c center positive. Use max(0).
`loss_base = cmax + log(zsum)/beta`. If cmax=0 and zsum? fine.

One subtlety: The logsumexp `z = beta*(c - cmax)`. If cmax set 0 due negative tiny but c values negative, z negative, okay. If cmax is zero but there are positive? no.

Run_lbfgs global best update: It may update global for a theta that is not local best for that n? It's exact, okay. But when calling from later n with same G_N? Works.

Potential problem: In L-BFGS, the gradient computed as `a*(grad_a-dot)`. If `dot` large? dot = sum a_i grad_a_i =2∑w_s c_s = 2m if w only active? With logsumexp sum weight=1, ∑w_s c_s maybe around max? ≤m. So dot ~2m. grad_a_i ~O(2m/n) +/-; diff O. Good.

Now, `run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(theta0, n, fsize, beta, maxiter, anchor=None):
    global G_N,...
    x0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x0.size != n:
       # interpolate a?
       a = theta_to_a(x0, x0.size)
       x0 = array_to_theta(a, G_CLIP)
    if anchor is None:
       anchor = int(np.argmax(x0))
       if anchor >= n: anchor=0
    x0 = x0 - x0[anchor]
    np.clip(x0, -G_CLIP, G_CLIP, out=x0)
    x0[anchor] = 0.0
    G_N=n; G_FSIZE=fsize; G_BETA=float(beta); G_CLIP=60.0; G_ANCHOR=anchor
    bounds = [(-G_CLIP, G_CLIP)] * n
    bounds[anchor] = (0.0, 0.0)
    options = {'maxiter': int(max(1,maxiter)), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 10, 'disp': False}
    try:
        res = minimize(theta_loss_grad, x0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
    except Exception as e:
        print("L-BFGS failed:", e)
        return x0
    if hasattr(res, 'x') and res.x is not None:
       return np.asarray(res.x, float)
    return x0
```
Note: `bounds = [(-G_CLIP, G_CLIP)] * n` uses same tuple object, okay. But anchor tuple (0,0) distinct. L-BFGS may modify? no.

If x0 size not n and we call array_to_theta, anchor chosen there; but run_lbfgs will choose anchor again maybe different, okay.

`G_CLIP` global used; set 60.0. `array_to_theta` default 60.

```
def make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr=None):
    inits=[]
    # parent
    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 1:
       p = np.asarray(parent_arr, float).reshape(-1)
       p = np.clip(p,0,1000)
       if p.sum() > 0:
          if p.size == n:
             ap = p
          else:
             old = np.linspace(0,1,p.size)
             new = np.linspace(0,1,n)
             ap = np.interp(new, old, p)
          ap = ap + 1e-12
          add_init(inits, ap, 60)
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # exponents
    for alpha in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
       a = np.exp(alpha*(x-0.5)); add_init
       a = np.exp(alpha*(0.5-x)); add_init
    # beta distributions
    for p in (0.3,0.7,1.5):
       for q in (0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0):
          if abs(p-q)>0.2 or ...: a=(x+1e-3)**p * (1-x+1e-3)**q; add
          # maybe cap 20
    # gaussians
    for mu in (0.25,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.75):
       for sigma in (0.12,0.25,0.45):
          a = 0.05 + np.exp(-((x-mu)**2)/(2*sigma*sigma)); add
    # inverse triangular / tent
    for mu in (0.3,0.5,0.7):
       for width in (0.25,0.5,0.8):
          a = np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - np.abs(x-mu)/width) + 0.02; add
    # flat spectrum 1/sqrt(k)
    k = np.arange(1,n+1)
    a = 1.0/np.sqrt(k); add; a = a[::-1]; add
    a = 1.0/(1.0 + k*0.05); add reverse
    # random theta smooth
    for _ in range(4):
       th = rng.normal(0,0.25,n)
       # simple low pass by moving average
       kernel = np.ones(5)/5? convolution? Or FFT low pass.
       # Use FFT if n power? simpler: np.update? Avoid loops. Use scipy.signal? not import. Use FFT:
       spec = fft(th, n) # if n maybe not power but fft works
       m = n//2
       spec[m:] = 0.0
       # also set lower m/3? leave.
       th = np.real(ifft(spec))
       # anchor max
       anchor = int(np.argmax(th))
       th = th - th[anchor]
       th = np.clip(th, -60, 60); th[anchor]=0
       inits.append(th)
    # random Gaussian mixture
    for _ in range(3):
       mu1 = rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); mu2 = rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); sig=...; a=0.05+mix; add
    # Limit inits: parent first, then diversity. Return maybe inits[:36]
    return inits
```

def search_for_n(n, max_seconds, rng, parent_arr):
    start = time.monotonic(); deadline= start+max_seconds
    if n < 2: return
    fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    local_obj = G_BEST_OBJ if G_BEST_N == n else inf
    local_a = G_BEST_A.copy() if G_BEST_A is not None and G_BEST_N == n else None
    local_theta = None
    if local_a is not None:
       local_theta = array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)
    inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr)
    if local_theta is not None:
       inits.insert(0, local_theta)
    if len(inits) > 32: inits = inits[:32]
    if not inits: inits=[np.zeros(n)]
    results = [(local_obj, local_theta)] if local_theta is not None else []
    scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0/float(n)))
    maxit1 = int(max(20, 100*scale))
    maxit2 = int(max(30, 200*scale))
    beta1 = 2000.0
    for idx, th0 in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       th = run_lbfgs(th0, n, fsize, beta1, maxit1)
       obj, a = exact_obj_from_theta(th, n, fsize)
       if obj < local_obj:
          local_obj=obj; local_a=a.copy(); local_theta=th.copy()
       results.append((obj, th))
       # Optionally update global? L-BFGS already. But if local obj from final not global? update local.
       # if idx small and time left? no.
    if local_theta is None and results:
       # take best result
    # sort results
    if len(results)>1:
       results.sort(key=tuple_first)
    # Phase 2: top 4
    top = min(4, len(results))
    for i in range(top):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
       th = results[i][1]
       for beta, miter in ((20000, maxit2), (100000, maxit2)):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
          th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, miter)
          obj,a=exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
          if obj < local_obj:
             local_obj=obj; local_a=a.copy(); local_theta=th.copy()
          # also if global is better for n, update
          if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_OBJ < local_obj:
             local_obj=G_BEST_OBJ; local_a=G_BEST_A.copy(); local_theta=G_BEST_THETA.copy()
       # if global n better too
    # Phase 3 random restarts from local
    if local_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
       for sigma in (0.3,0.18,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.012):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
          th_noisy = local_theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
          anchor = int(np.argmax(th_noisy))
          th = run_lbfgs(th_noisy, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2, anchor=anchor)
          obj,a=exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
          if obj < local_obj: ...
    # Phase 4 LP
    if local_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and n <= 16384:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       lp_time = min(max(10.0, rem*0.35), 240.0)
       Kmax = 96 if n <= 8192 else 64
       best_a, best_obj = lp_descent(local_a, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, 24, Kmax)
       if best_obj < local_obj:
          local_obj=best_obj; local_a=best_a.copy(); local_theta=array_to_theta(best_a,60)
          # final L-BFGS from LP
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
             th2=run_lbfgs(local_theta, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2)
             obj2,a2=exact_obj_from_theta(th2,n,fsize)
             if obj2 < local_obj: update
    if local_a is not None and local_obj < G_BEST_OBJ:
       G_BEST_OBJ=local_obj; G_BEST_N=n; G_BEST_A=local_a.copy(); G_BEST_THETA=local_theta if local_theta is not None else array_to_theta(local_a,60)
```

def lp_descent(a, n, fsize, max_time, rng, Kinit=24, Kmax=128):
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA
    a = np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return a,inf
    a/=s
    c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=max(0,c.max()); obj=2*n*m
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    if obj < G_BEST_OBJ - 1e-14: update global
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    K=Kinit; stall=0; U=50.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1 and K <= Kmax and stall < 10:
       c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=float(max(0,np.max(c))); obj=2*n*m
       if obj < best_obj: best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); update global
       # active
       if m <= 0: break

          cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])

          part = np.argpartition(c, Kmax-1)[::-1][:Kmax] # top Kmax
          if part.size==0: part=np.array([np.argmax(c)])
          cand = part
       if cand.size > K:
          cand = cand[:K]?? Need top K among cand.

Now, one mathematical improvement: Since the objective for normalized sum1 is `2n*cmax`. In L-BFGS with logsumexp, loss `factor*(cmax + logZ/beta)`. If beta high, `logZ/beta` is negative small; minimizing Lβ might not exactly minimize cmax if active set changes? As β→∞, Lβ→cmax - log(K_active)/β. The `-log(1/K)/β` small but could prefer fewer active peaks? v? It tends to cmax. Good.

Maybe beta=100000 with cmax ~1e-4 gives β*cmax=10, β*low maybe 0 for endpoints; logZ ~log(number with near max). For endpoints, exp(-10)=4e-5, not negligible; active weights include many lower peaks with weight 4e-5. Good. For beta=1e6, endpoints weight e^-100~0, concentrated top. Maybe use 100000. Good.

def add_init(inits, a, clip=60.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < 8:
        return
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if np.any(~np.isfinite(a)):
        a[np.isnan(a)] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return
    inits.append(array_to_theta(a, clip))
```

def add_random_theta(inits, n, rng, clip=60.0):
    th = rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)
    # low-pass: use FFT size n (n power); if n not power, okay.
    try:
       spec = fft(th, n)
       m = n // 2
       # zero top m maybe and high
       spec[m:] = 0.0
       # keep low frequencies: set high half zero
       th = float(np.real(ifft(spec)).max?)
    except Exception:
       pass
    # also maybe add low-frequency trend
    th += 0.2*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(0.1,1.0,n)?) # Avoid vector random with n? Use rng.uniform scalar.
    th += 0.2 * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*(rng.uniform(0,1) + np.linspace(0,1,n)))
    anchor = int(np.argmax(th))
    th = th - th[anchor]
    np.clip(th, -clip, clip, out=th)
    th[anchor]=0.0
    inits.append(th.astype(np.float64))
```

def polish_global(rng, deadline):
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA
    if G_BEST_A is None: return
    noise_sigs = [0.0, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004]
    lp_counter = 0
    for it in range(100):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25: break
        n = int(G_BEST_N)
        fsize = next_pow2(2*n-1)
        theta = array_to_theta(G_BEST_A, 60.0)
        scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0 / n))
        miter = int(max(20, 120*scale))
        sigma = noise_sigs[it % len(noise_sigs)]
        if sigma > 0:
            theta_noisy = theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            anchor = int(np.argmax(theta_noisy))
            theta_noisy = theta_noisy - theta_noisy[anchor]
            np.clip(theta_noisy, -60.0, 60.0, out=theta_noisy)
            theta_noisy[anchor] = 0.0
        else:
            anchor = int(np.argmax(theta))
        beta = 100000.0 if it >= 2 else 20000.0
        if rem > 30:
            theta = run_lbfgs(theta_noisy if sigma else theta, n, fsize, beta, miter, anchor=anchor)
            obj,a = exact_obj_from_theta(theta,n,fsize)
            if obj < G_BEST_OBJ - 1e-12: update global
        # LP every 3rd iteration if time and n not too large
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 100 and n <= 16384 and (it % 3 == 0):
            lp_time = min(90.0 + 5.0*it, rem*0.5)
            best_a, best_obj = lp_descent(G_BEST_A, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, 24, 96)
            if best_obj < G_BEST_OBJ:
                # current global updated inside; then L-BFGS
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
                    th = array_to_theta(G_BEST_A, 60.0)
                    run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, 100000.0, miter)
        # If no improvement and many iterations, decrease? not track.
```

```
"""..."""
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

G_N = 0
G_FSIZE = 1
G_BETA = 1.0
G_CLIP = 60.0
G_ANCHOR = 0
G_BEST_OBJ = float("inf")
G_BEST_N = 0
G_BEST_A = None
G_BEST_THETA = None

def next_pow2(m): ...

def convolve_self(a, n=None, fsize=None): ...

def theta_to_a(theta, n=None):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n is None: n = th.size
    if th.size != n:
        # if mismatch, interpolate? Use theta_to_a on size? But caller ensures.
        if th.size == 1: th = np.repeat(th, n)
        else:
            old = np.linspace(0,1,th.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
            th = np.interp(new, old, th)
    th = np.clip(th, -80.0, 80.0)
    if n == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    mx = float(np.max(th))
    e = np.exp(th - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n))
    return e / s
```
Need if th empty? n maybe 0? Not.

`array_to_theta`:
```
def array_to_theta(a, clip=60.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(a.size, dtype=float)
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    anchor = int(np.argmax(a))
    with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        th = np.log(a) - np.log(a[anchor])
    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
    th = np.clip(th, -clip, clip)
    th[anchor] = 0.0
    return th.astype(np.float64)
```

`exact_obj_from_theta`:
```
def exact_obj_from_theta(theta, n, fsize):
    a=theta_to_a(theta,n)
    c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize)
    obj = 2.0*float(n)*max(0.0,float(c.max()))
    return obj,a
```

`exact_obj_from_a` similar.

Now, `run_lbfgs` anchor: If theta0 size not n, we call array_to_theta then choose anchor. But if theta0 from local is full length n, good.

```
def run_lbfgs(theta0, n, fsize, beta, maxiter, anchor=None):
    global G_N, G_FSIZE, G_BETA, G_CLIP, G_ANCHOR
    n=int(n)
    x0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if x0.size != n:
        a = theta_to_a(x0, x0.size)
        x0 = array_to_theta(a, 60.0)
    if x0.size < 1:
        return x0
    if anchor is None:
        anchor = int(np.argmax(x0))
        if anchor < 0 or anchor >= n: anchor = 0
    x0 = x0 - float(x0[anchor])
    np.clip(x0, -60.0, 60.0, out=x0)
    x0[anchor] = 0.0
    G_N = n; G_FSIZE = int(fsize); G_BETA = float(beta); G_CLIP = 60.0; G_ANCHOR = int(anchor)
    bounds = [(-G_CLIP, G_CLIP)] * n
    bounds[anchor] = (0.0, 0.0)
    options = {"maxiter": int(max(1, maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 10, "disp": False}
    try:
       res = minimize(theta_loss_grad, x0, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=options)
    except Exception as exc:
       print("L-BFGS-B exception:", exc)
       return x0
    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
       return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    return x0
```
Note `bounds = [tuple] * n` uses same tuple; if n=0? no.

Potential problem: L-BFGS-B with anchor bound (0,0) and x0 anchor 0, but line search may try values slightly outside and project. Good.

Now, `lp_build_matrix` top-level:
```
def lp_build_matrix(a, n, active, cm):
    K = int(active.size)
    if K == 0: return None
    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    inv = 1.0 / float(cm) if cm > 0 else 1.0
    for r, s in enumerate(active):
       s = int(s)
       lo = max(0, s - (n - 1))
       hi = min(n - 1, s)
       if hi < lo: continue
       start = s - hi
       end = s - lo + 1
       A[r, lo:hi+1] = a[start:end][::-1] * inv
    return A
```
If active integer array, `.size`.

`lp_descent` detailed code. Need `global` for global updates.
```
def lp_descent(a, n, fsize, max_time, rng, Kinit=24, Kmax=128):
    global G_BEST_OBJ,...
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return a, inf
    a=a/s
    c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=max(0,float(c.max())); obj=2*n*m
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    if obj < G_BEST_OBJ -1e-15: update
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    K=int(Kinit); stall=0; LP_U=10.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5 and K <= Kmax and stall < 10:
        c=convolve_self(a,n,fsize); m=max(0,float(c.max())); obj=2*n*m
        if obj < best_obj:
            best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy()
            if obj < G_BEST_OBJ -1e-15: global...
        if m <= 0 or stall >= 10: break
        use_k = min(K, c.size)
        if use_k >= c.size:
            active = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, c.size - use_k)[-use_k:]
        A = lp_build_matrix(a,n,active,m)
        if A is None or A.size == 0:
            stall += 1; K = min(Kmax, max(K+5, K*2)); continue
        c_obj = -np.ones(n)
        b_ub = np.ones(active.size) * (1.0 + 1e-7)
        bounds = [(0.0, LP_U)] * n
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
            if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
                raise RuntimeError("linprog failed")
        except Exception as exc:
            # Do not try simplex to avoid long; maybe try with bigger tolerance?
            stall += 2
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 4, K * 2)))
            continue
        b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
        bs = float(b.sum())
        if bs <= 1e-12:
            stall += 1; K = min(Kmax, max(K+4, K*2)); continue
        b = b / bs
        # line search
        best_t_obj = np.inf; best_cand = None
        for t in (0.25,0.5,0.4,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 0: continue
            cand /= cs
            c2 = convolve_self(cand,n,fsize)
            obj2 = 2.0 * float(n) * max(0.0, float(c2.max()))
            if obj2 < best_t_obj:
                best_t_obj = obj2; best_cand = cand
        if best_cand is not None and best_t_obj < obj - 1e-14:
            a = best_cand
            stall = 0
            # objective improved; K increase slightly
            K = int(min(Kmax, K + max(4, K // 6)))
        else:
            stall += 1
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 6, K * 2)))
    if best_obj < G_BEST_OBJ -1e-15:
       update_global_direct(best_a,n,best_obj)
    return best_a, best_obj
```
Need avoid `not res.success` if res.success missing? HiGHS res has. Good.

But `res.success` may be True even if no improvement; okay.

One issue: `bounds = [(0.0, LP_U)] * n` with n=100k would memory; n≤16384. Good.

`A` matrix if K=1, active size 1; b_ub length1. LP with one constraint many vars can solve; optimum may push all vars with small coefficient. Good.

If `active.size` 0, skip. `use_k` maybe 0 if K=0? Kinit positive.

Potential optimization: If `best_t_obj < obj` but very small improvement, reset stall. Good.

Code exact:
```
def search_for_n(n, max_seconds, rng, parent_arr):
    global ...
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    n=int(n)
    if n < 8 or max_seconds <= 0: return G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_A
    fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    local_obj = G_BEST_OBJ if G_BEST_N == n else float('inf')
    local_a = G_BEST_A.copy() if G_BEST_A is not None and G_BEST_N == n else None
    local_theta = G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None and G_BEST_N == n else None
    if local_theta is None and local_a is not None:
       local_theta = array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)
    inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr)
    if local_theta is not None:
       inits.insert(0, local_theta)
    if len(inits) > 32:
       inits = inits[:32]
    if not inits:
       inits = [np.zeros(n, dtype=float)]
    results = []
    if local_theta is not None:
       results.append((local_obj, local_theta))
    scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0 / float(n)))
    maxit1 = int(max(20, 100 * scale))
    maxit2 = int(max(30, 220 * scale))
    for idx, th0 in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       th = run_lbfgs(th0, n, fsize, 2000.0, maxit1)
       obj,a = exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
       if obj < local_obj:
          local_obj=obj; local_a=a.copy(); local_theta=th.copy()
       if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-14:
          local_obj=G_BEST_OBJ; local_a=G_BEST_A.copy(); local_theta=G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a,60.0)
       results.append((obj, th))
    if results:
       results.sort(key=tuple_first)
    top = min(4, len(results))
    for i in range(top):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
       th = results[i][1]
       for beta, miter in ((20000.0, maxit2), (100000.0, maxit2)):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
           th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, miter)
           obj,a = exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
           if obj < local_obj:
              local_obj=obj; local_a=a.copy(); local_theta=th.copy()
           if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-14:
              local_obj=G_BEST_OBJ; local_a=G_BEST_A.copy(); local_theta=G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a,60.0)
    if local_theta is None and results:
       local_obj = results[0][0]; local_theta = results[0][1]; local_a = theta_to_a(local_theta,n)
    if local_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
       for sigma in (0.3, 0.18, 0.1, 0.05, 0.025, 0.012):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
           th_noisy = local_theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
           anchor = int(np.argmax(th_noisy))
           th = run_lbfgs(th_noisy, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2, anchor=anchor)
           obj,a = exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
           if obj < local_obj:
              local_obj=obj; local_a=a.copy(); local_theta=th.copy()
           if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-14:
              local_obj=G_BEST_OBJ; local_a=G_BEST_A.copy(); local_theta=G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a,60.0)
    if local_a is not None and n <= 16384 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       lp_time = max(10.0, min(240.0, rem * 0.35))
       kmax = 96 if n <= 8192 else 64
       best_a, best_obj = lp_descent(local_a, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, 24, kmax)
       if best_obj < local_obj:
          local_obj=best_obj; local_a=best_a.copy(); local_theta=array_to_theta(best_a,60.0)
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
             th2 = run_lbfgs(local_theta, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2)
             obj2,a2 = exact_obj_from_theta(th2,n,fsize)
             if obj2 < local_obj:
                local_obj=obj2; local_a=a2.copy(); local_theta=th2.copy()
    if local_a is not None and local_obj < G_BEST_OBJ:
       update_global_direct(local_a, n, local_obj)
    return local_obj, local_a
```
Potential bug: In `if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-14:` when local_a None? If G_BEST_A not None. If G_BEST_N==n, G_BEST_A not None. Good.

Now, `polish_global` uses `update_global_direct`. It may call `lp_descent` with `G_BEST_A` that is improved inside. To use improved A after L-BFGS, while loop next. Good.

```
def polish_global(rng, deadline):
    global ...
    if G_BEST_A is None: return
    sigmas = (0.0, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002)
    for it in range(200):
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 30: break
       n=int(G_BEST_N); fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1)
       scale=max(0.25,min(2.0,8192.0/n))
       miter=int(max(20,140*scale))
       theta = array_to_theta(G_BEST_A,60.0)
       sigma = sigmas[it % len(sigmas)]
       if sigma > 0:
          th = theta + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
          anchor=int(np.argmax(th))
          th = th - th[anchor]
          np.clip(th,-60.0,60.0,out=th); th[anchor]=0.0
       else:
          th = theta
          anchor=None
       beta=20000.0 if it < 3 else 100000.0
       if rem > 35:
          th = run_lbfgs(th,n,fsize,beta,miter,anchor=anchor)
          obj,a=exact_obj_from_theta(th,n,fsize)
          update_global_direct(a,n,obj)
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem > 120 and n <= 16384 and (it % 2 == 0):
          lp_t = min(90.0 + 3.0*it, rem*0.45)
          best_a,best_obj=lp_descent(G_BEST_A,n,fsize,lp_t,rng,24,80)
          update_global_direct(best_a,n,best_obj)
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
             th2=array_to_theta(G_BEST_A,60.0)
             th2=run_lbfgs(th2,n,fsize,100000.0,miter)
             obj2,a2=exact_obj_from_theta(th2,n,fsize); update_global_direct(a2,n,obj2)
```

def build_final_list():
    global G_BEST_A
    if G_BEST_A is not None and G_BEST_A.size >= 2:
       a=np.asarray(G_BEST_A,float).reshape(-1)
       a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
       s=a.sum()
       if s > 0:
          a=a/s
          if a.size <= 16384:
             return [float(x) for x in a]
    a=np.ones(1024); a/=a.sum()
    return [float(x) for x in a]

Then in propose:
```
final_list = build_final_list()
try:
    ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    print("Verified evaluate_sequence:", ver)
except Exception as e:
    print("evaluate_sequence not usable:", e)
    # If exception due sequence invalid? try fallback
    final_list = [1.0/1024.0]*1024
    try: ver=evaluate_sequence(final_list); print...
    except: ver=None
return final_list
```

def add_random_theta(inits, n, rng, clip=60.0):
    n=int(n)
    th = rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)
    try:
        spec = fft(th, n)
        m = n // 2
        if m > 1:
            spec[m:] = 0.0
            m2 = max(1, n // 4)
            spec[m2:m] = 0.0  # more low-pass
        th = np.real(ifft(spec))
    except Exception:
        pass
    phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
    th = th + 0.2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (phase + np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)))
    anchor = int(np.argmax(th))
    th = th - float(th[anchor])
    np.clip(th, -clip, clip, out=th)
    th[anchor] = 0.0
    inits.append(th.astype(np.float64))
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The code combines:
  * fast FFT convolution and log-sum-exp smoothed objectives,
  * L-BFGS-B optimization in a softmax/log parameterization,
  * multi-scale / multi-initialization search,
  * a cutting-plane linear-programming pincer step inspired by the old
    state-of-the-art construction,
  * final local polishing and random restarts.

The function propose_candidate() returns the best valid list found under
the supplied time budget.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

# Globals used by the L-BFGS objective gradient pair.
G_N = 0
G_FSIZE = 1
G_BETA = 1.0
G_CLIP = 60.0
G_ANCHOR = 0

# Best solution found anywhere in the current run.
G_BEST_OBJ = float("inf")
G_BEST_N = 0
G_BEST_A = None
G_BEST_THETA = None


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m (m positive)."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def convolve_self(a, n=None, fsize=None):
    """Full self-convolution of a real array a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n is None:
        n = a.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = next_pow2(size)
    else:
        fsize = int(fsize)
    if fsize < size:
        fsize = next_pow2(size)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def theta_to_a(theta, n=None):
    """Convert log-softmax parameters theta to a non-negative unit-sum array."""
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if n is None:
        n = th.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if th.size != n:
        if th.size == 0:
            th = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        elif th.size == 1:
            th = np.full(n, float(th[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, th.size)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            th = np.interp(new, old, th)

    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
    th = np.clip(th, -80.0, 80.0)
    mx = float(np.max(th))
    e = np.exp(th - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def array_to_theta(a, clip=60.0):
    """Convert a non-negative array to log-softmax parameters with max anchored at 0."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)

    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    anchor = int(np.argmax(a))
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        th = np.log(a) - np.log(a[anchor])
    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
    th = np.clip(th, -float(clip), float(clip))
    th[anchor] = 0.0
    return th.astype(np.float64)


def exact_obj_from_theta(theta, n, fsize):
    """Exact objective and corresponding unit-sum array from theta parameters."""
    a = theta_to_a(theta, n)
    c = convolve_self(a, int(n), fsize)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    return obj, a


def exact_obj_from_a(a, n, fsize):
    """Exact objective for a (it is normalized internally)."""
    a = theta_to_a(a, n)  # normalize via softmax; if a is already unit-sum this is stable.
    c = convolve_self(a, int(n), fsize)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    return obj, a


def update_global_direct(a, n, obj):
    """Update global best from an already-evaluated unit-sum array."""
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return
    a = a / s
    if np.isfinite(obj) and float(obj) < float(G_BEST_OBJ) - 1e-15:
        G_BEST_OBJ = float(obj)
        G_BEST_N = n
        G_BEST_A = a
        G_BEST_THETA = array_to_theta(a, 60.0)


def theta_loss_grad(x):
    """
    Value and gradient of
        2n * (1/beta) * log(sum exp(beta*c_s))
    where c_s are the self-convolution coefficients of softmax(x).
    """
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA

    n = int(G_N)
    fsize = int(G_FSIZE)
    beta = float(G_BETA)
    anchor = int(G_ANCHOR)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    th = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if th.size != n:
        th = array_to_theta(th, 60.0)
    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
    th = np.clip(th, -float(G_CLIP), float(G_CLIP))
    th[anchor] = 0.0

    mx = float(np.max(th))
    e = np.exp(th - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = e / s
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        ss = float(np.sum(a))
        if ss > 0.0:
            a = a / ss

    A = fft(a, fsize)
    csize = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:csize]
    cmax = max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    exact = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < G_BEST_OBJ - 1e-14:
        G_BEST_OBJ = float(exact)
        G_BEST_N = n
        G_BEST_A = a.copy()
        G_BEST_THETA = th.copy()

    # Stable log-sum-exp for the smoothed maximum.
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    zsum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not np.isfinite(zsum) or zsum <= 0.0:
        loss_base = cmax
        w = np.zeros(csize, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        loss_base = cmax + np.log(zsum) / beta
        w = ez / zsum

    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_a = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_th = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_th[anchor] = 0.0
    grad_th = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_th), grad_th, 0.0)

    factor = 2.0 * float(n)
    return float(factor * loss_base), factor * grad_th


def run_lbfgs(theta0, n, fsize, beta, maxiter, anchor=None):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on theta parameters for a fixed n and FFT size."""
    global G_N, G_FSIZE, G_BETA, G_CLIP, G_ANCHOR

    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()

    x0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if x0.size != n:
        a = theta_to_a(x0, x0.size)
        x0 = array_to_theta(a, 60.0)

    if anchor is None:
        anchor = int(np.argmax(x0)) if x0.size else 0
        if anchor < 0 or anchor >= n:
            anchor = 0
    anchor = int(anchor)

    x0 = x0 - float(x0[anchor])
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -60.0, 60.0)
    x0[anchor] = 0.0

    G_N = n
    G_FSIZE = int(fsize)
    G_BETA = float(beta)
    G_CLIP = 60.0
    G_ANCHOR = anchor

    bounds = [(-60.0, 60.0)] * n
    bounds[anchor] = (0.0, 0.0)

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(max(1, maxiter)),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 10,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            theta_loss_grad,
            x0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        print("L-BFGS-B exception:", repr(exc))
        return x0

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
        out = np.clip(out, -60.0, 60.0)
        out[anchor] = 0.0
        return out
    return x0


def tuple_first(pair):
    """Helper for sorting (objective, theta) pairs without lambda."""
    return pair[0]


def add_init(inits, a, clip=60.0):
    """Add a positive array as a log-softmax initialization."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < 8:
        return
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return
    inits.append(array_to_theta(a, clip))


def add_random_theta(inits, n, rng, clip=60.0):
    """Add a low-pass smooth random log-parameter initialization."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 8:
        return
    th = rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)
    try:
        spec = fft(th, n)
        m = n // 2
        if m > 1:
            m2 = max(1, n // 4)
            if m2 < m:
                spec[m2:m] = 0.0
            spec[m:] = 0.0
        th = np.real(ifft(spec))
    except Exception:
        pass

    phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
    th = th + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (phase + np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)))
    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)

    anchor = int(np.argmax(th))
    th = th - float(th[anchor])
    th = np.clip(th, -float(clip), float(clip))
    th[anchor] = 0.0
    inits.append(th.astype(np.float64))


def make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr):
    """Build a diverse but manageable list of theta initializations for length n."""
    inits = []
    n = int(n)
    if n < 8:
        return [np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    if parent_arr is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if p.size > 1 and s > 0.0:
            if p.size == n:
                ap = p
            else:
                old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
                new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                ap = np.interp(new, old, p)
            ap = ap + 1e-12
            add_init(inits, ap, 60.0)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = 1e-3

    # Exponential ramps, both directions.
    for alpha in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
        add_init(inits, np.exp(alpha * (x - 0.5)), 60.0)
        add_init(inits, np.exp(alpha * (0.5 - x)), 60.0)

    # Asymmetric beta-like shapes.
    for p in (0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0):
        for q in (0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0):
            if abs(float(p) - float(q)) <= 0.01:
                continue
            add_init(inits, (x + eps) ** p * (1.0 - x + eps) ** q, 60.0)

    # Off-center Gaussians and skewed mixtures.
    for mu in (0.25, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.75):
        for sig in (0.12, 0.25, 0.45):
            add_init(inits, 0.03 + np.exp(-((x - mu) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig)), 60.0)

    for _ in range(3):
        mu1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        mu2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.45))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7))
        a = (
            0.03
            + w * np.exp(-((x - mu1) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            + (1.0 - w) * np.exp(-((x - mu2) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        )
        add_init(inits, a, 60.0)

    # Tent functions.
    for mu in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7):
        for width in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8):
            add_init(inits, 0.03 + np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - np.abs(x - mu) / width), 60.0)

    # Flatter-spectrum inspired decays.
    k = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    add_init(inits, 1.0 / np.sqrt(k) + 0.01, 60.0)
    add_init(inits, 1.0 / np.sqrt(k)[::-1] + 0.01, 60.0)
    add_init(inits, 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.05 * k) + 0.01, 60.0)
    add_init(inits, (1.0 / (1.0 + 0.05 * k))[::-1] + 0.01, 60.0)

    # Smooth random spectra.
    for _ in range(4):
        add_random_theta(inits, n, rng, 60.0)

    if len(inits) > 34:
        inits = inits[:34]
    if not inits:
        inits = [np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    return inits


def lp_build_matrix(a, n, active, cm):
    """
    Build the active constraint matrix A for the LP pincer.
    Row s is a[m] * a[s-m] scaled by 1/cm.
    """
    K = int(np.asarray(active).size)
    n = int(n)
    if K <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    inv = 1.0 / float(cm) if float(cm) > 0.0 else 1.0
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    for r, s in enumerate(active):
        s = int(s)
        lo = max(0, s - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, s)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        start = s - hi
        end = s - lo + 1
        A[r, lo:hi + 1] = a[start:end][::-1] * inv
    return A


def lp_descent(a, n, fsize, max_time, rng, Kinit=24, Kmax=128):
    """
    LP pincer descent.

    Given current a and current max M, solve the LP
        maximize sum(b)
        subject to 0 <= b <= U and cross(a,b)_s <= M for top active s.
    Then try small convex combinations a -> (1-t)a + t*b.
    """
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA

    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or max_time <= 0.0:
        return a, G_BEST_OBJ

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return a, float("inf")
    a = a / s

    c = convolve_self(a, n, fsize)
    m = max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
    best_obj = 2.0 * float(n) * m
    best_a = a.copy()
    update_global_direct(best_a, n, best_obj)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_time)
    K = int(max(4, Kinit))
    Kmax = int(min(Kmax, max(K, 128)))
    stall = 0
    LP_U = 10.0

    print("LP descent: n=%d, Kinit=%d, Kmax=%d, time=%.1f" % (n, K, Kmax, max_time))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5 and K <= Kmax and stall < 12:
        c = convolve_self(a, n, fsize)
        m = max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
        obj = 2.0 * float(n) * m

        if obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()
            update_global_direct(best_a, n, best_obj)
            print("  LP improved: %.8f" % best_obj)

        if m <= 0.0:
            break

        use_k = min(int(K), c.size)
        if use_k >= c.size:
            active = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, c.size - use_k)[-use_k:]

        A = lp_build_matrix(a, n, active, m)
        if A is None or A.size == 0:
            stall += 1
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 6, K * 2)))
            continue

        cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bub = np.ones(active.size, dtype=np.float64) * (1.0 + 1e-7)
        bounds = [(0.0, LP_U)] * n

        ok = False
        b = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                ok = True
        except Exception as exc:
            # Do not fall back to a potentially much slower simplex method;
            # the smoothed L-BFGS pipeline remains the main workhorse.
            print("  LP solve failed, skipping:", repr(exc))

        if ok:
            b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
            b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
            bs = float(np.sum(b))
            if bs > 1e-12:
                b = b / bs
                best_t_obj = float("inf")
                best_cand = None
                for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.4, 0.15, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005):
                    cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
                    cs = float(np.sum(cand))
                    if cs <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    cand = cand / cs
                    c2 = convolve_self(cand, n, fsize)
                    obj2 = 2.0 * float(n) * max(0.0, float(np.max(c2)))
                    if obj2 < best_t_obj:
                        best_t_obj = obj2
                        best_cand = cand

                if best_cand is not None and best_t_obj < obj - 1e-14:
                    a = best_cand
                    stall = 0
                    K = int(min(Kmax, K + max(4, K // 6)))
                else:
                    stall += 1
                    K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 8, K * 2)))
            else:
                stall += 1
                K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 8, K * 2)))
        else:
            stall += 2
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(K + 8, K * 2)))

    update_global_direct(best_a, n, best_obj)
    print("  LP finished: best=%.8f" % best_obj)
    return best_a, best_obj


def search_for_n(n, max_seconds, rng, parent_arr):
    """Search a fixed length n for up to max_seconds."""
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA

    n = int(n)
    if n < 64 or max_seconds <= 0.0:
        return G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_A

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max_seconds)
    fsize = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    print("=== search_for_n n=%d max_seconds=%.1f fsize=%d ===" % (n, max_seconds, fsize))

    local_obj = G_BEST_OBJ if (G_BEST_N == n and np.isfinite(G_BEST_OBJ)) else float("inf")
    local_a = None
    local_theta = None

    if G_BEST_N == n and G_BEST_A is not None:
        local_a = np.asarray(G_BEST_A, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        local_theta = G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)
        local_obj = float(G_BEST_OBJ)

    inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent_arr)
    if local_theta is not None:
        inits.insert(0, local_theta)
    if len(inits) > 34:
        inits = inits[:34]
    if not inits:
        inits = [np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    results = []
    if local_theta is not None:
        results.append((local_obj, local_theta))

    scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0 / float(n)))
    maxit1 = int(max(20, 90 * scale))
    maxit2 = int(max(30, 200 * scale))

    # Phase 1: broad smoothed optimization from many initializations.
    for idx, th0 in enumerate(inits):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        th = run_lbfgs(th0, n, fsize, 2000.0, maxit1)
        obj, a = exact_obj_from_theta(th, n, fsize)
        update_global_direct(a, n, obj)

        if obj < local_obj - 1e-15:
            local_obj = obj
            local_a = a.copy()
            local_theta = th.copy()

        if G_BEST_N == n and np.isfinite(G_BEST_OBJ) and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-13:
            local_obj = float(G_BEST_OBJ)
            local_a = G_BEST_A.copy()
            local_theta = G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)

        results.append((obj, th))

    if local_theta is not None:
        results.append((local_obj, local_theta))

    if results:
        results.sort(key=tuple_first)

    # Phase 2: continue the best few with sharper log-sum-exp.
    top = min(4, len(results))
    for i in range(top):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        th = results[i][1]
        for beta, miter in ((20000.0, maxit2), (100000.0, maxit2)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, miter)
            obj, a = exact_obj_from_theta(th, n, fsize)
            update_global_direct(a, n, obj)

            if obj < local_obj - 1e-15:
                local_obj = obj
                local_a = a.copy()
                local_theta = th.copy()

            if G_BEST_N == n and np.isfinite(G_BEST_OBJ) and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-13:
                local_obj = float(G_BEST_OBJ)
                local_a = G_BEST_A.copy()
                local_theta = G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)

    if local_theta is None and results:
        local_obj = results[0][0]
        local_theta = results[0][1]
        local_a = theta_to_a(local_theta, n)

    # Phase 3: small random perturbations from the local best.
    if local_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        for sigma in (0.30, 0.18, 0.10, 0.05, 0.025, 0.012):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 12.0:
                break
            th_noisy = local_theta + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n)
            anchor = int(np.argmax(th_noisy))
            th = run_lbfgs(th_noisy, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2, anchor=anchor)
            obj, a = exact_obj_from_theta(th, n, fsize)
            update_global_direct(a, n, obj)

            if obj < local_obj - 1e-15:
                local_obj = obj
                local_a = a.copy()
                local_theta = th.copy()

            if G_BEST_N == n and np.isfinite(G_BEST_OBJ) and G_BEST_OBJ <= local_obj + 1e-13:
                local_obj = float(G_BEST_OBJ)
                local_a = G_BEST_A.copy()
                local_theta = G_BEST_THETA.copy() if G_BEST_THETA is not None else array_to_theta(local_a, 60.0)

    # Phase 4: LP pincer polish.
    if local_a is not None and n <= 16384 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        lp_time = max(10.0, min(240.0, rem * 0.35))
        kmax = 96 if n <= 8192 else 64
        best_a, best_obj = lp_descent(local_a, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, 24, kmax)

        if best_obj < local_obj - 1e-15:
            local_obj = best_obj
            local_a = best_a.copy()
            local_theta = array_to_theta(best_a, 60.0)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
                th2 = run_lbfgs(local_theta, n, fsize, 100000.0, maxit2)
                obj2, a2 = exact_obj_from_theta(th2, n, fsize)
                update_global_direct(a2, n, obj2)
                if obj2 < local_obj - 1e-15:
                    local_obj = obj2
                    local_a = a2.copy()
                    local_theta = th2.copy()

    if local_a is not None and np.isfinite(local_obj):
        update_global_direct(local_a, n, local_obj)

    print("search_for_n n=%d finished: local=%.8f global=%.8f" %
          (n, local_obj if np.isfinite(local_obj) else float("inf"), G_BEST_OBJ))
    return local_obj, local_a


def polish_global(rng, deadline):
    """Use remaining time to polish the current global best."""
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA

    if G_BEST_A is None or G_BEST_N <= 0:
        return

    sigmas = (0.0, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002)
    print("=== global polish: starting n=%d obj=%.8f ===" % (G_BEST_N, G_BEST_OBJ))

    for it in range(200):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        n = int(G_BEST_N)
        fsize = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        scale = max(0.25, min(2.0, 8192.0 / float(n)))
        miter = int(max(20, 140 * scale))

        theta = array_to_theta(G_BEST_A, 60.0)
        sigma = sigmas[it % len(sigmas)]

        if sigma > 0.0:
            th = theta + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n)
            anchor = int(np.argmax(th))
            th = th - float(th[anchor])
            th = np.clip(th, -60.0, 60.0)
            th[anchor] = 0.0
        else:
            th = theta
            anchor = None

        beta = 20000.0 if it < 3 else 100000.0

        if rem > 35.0:
            th = run_lbfgs(th, n, fsize, beta, miter, anchor=anchor)
            obj, a = exact_obj_from_theta(th, n, fsize)
            update_global_direct(a, n, obj)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0 and n <= 16384 and (it % 2 == 0):
            lp_time = min(90.0 + 3.0 * float(it), 0.45 * float(rem))
            best_a, best_obj = lp_descent(G_BEST_A, n, fsize, lp_time, rng, 24, 80)
            update_global_direct(best_a, n, best_obj)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
                th2 = array_to_theta(G_BEST_A, 60.0)
                th2 = run_lbfgs(th2, n, fsize, 100000.0, miter)
                obj2, a2 = exact_obj_from_theta(th2, n, fsize)
                update_global_direct(a2, n, obj2)

        if it % 5 == 4:
            print("  polish iter=%d rem=%.1f global=%.8f" % (it, rem, G_BEST_OBJ))


def build_final_list():
    """Build a valid final list from the global best, with a safe fallback."""
    global G_BEST_A

    if G_BEST_A is not None:
        a = np.asarray(G_BEST_A, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
        if a.size >= 2 and a.size <= 32768:
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if s > 0.0:
                a = a / s
                if s >= 0.01 and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                    return [float(v) for v in a]

    # Very safe fallback: constants have objective exactly 2.
    a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point invoked by the harness.

    Returns a Python list of non-negative floats minimizing the evaluation
    objective as much as possible before the wall-clock budget.
    """
    global G_BEST_OBJ, G_BEST_N, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_THETA

    # Start fresh if this function is called multiple times in one process.
    G_BEST_OBJ = float("inf")
    G_BEST_N = 0
    G_BEST_A = None
    G_BEST_THETA = None

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start = time.monotonic()
    # Keep a comfortable buffer for final evaluation and return path.
    deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - 90.0)

    print("propose_candidate start; budget=%.1f internal_deadline=%.1f" % (budget_s, deadline - start))

    # Recover optional parent construction.
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        raw_parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw_parent is not None:
            parent_arr = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            parent_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(parent_arr), parent_arr, 0.0)
            parent_arr = np.clip(parent_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent_arr.size < 8 or float(parent_arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
                parent_arr = None
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    # Seed global best from parent if available, downsample if too large.
    if parent_arr is not None:
        psize = int(parent_arr.size)
        if psize > 16384:
            n_seed = 8192
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, psize)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_seed)
            a_seed = np.interp(new, old, parent_arr)
        else:
            n_seed = psize
            a_seed = parent_arr

        if a_seed.size >= 8:
            fsize = next_pow2(2 * int(a_seed.size) - 1)
            a_seed = np.clip(a_seed, 0.0, 1000.0)
            a_seed = np.where(np.isfinite(a_seed), a_seed, 0.0)
            s0 = float(a_seed.sum())
            if s0 > 0.0:
                a_seed = a_seed / s0
                c0 = convolve_self(a_seed, int(a_seed.size), fsize)
                obj0 = 2.0 * float(a_seed.size) * max(0.0, float(np.max(c0)))
                update_global_direct(a_seed, int(a_seed.size), obj0)
                print("parent seed: n=%d obj=%.8f" % (a_seed.size, obj0))

    # Determine main search length.
    if G_BEST_N >= 2048 and G_BEST_N <= 16384:
        main_n = int(G_BEST_N)
    else:
        main_n = 8192

    # Candidate lengths.
    candidates = []
    for nn in (main_n, 8192, 16384, 4096):
        nn = int(nn)
        if 2048 <= nn <= 16384 and nn not in candidates:
            candidates.append(nn)
    if not candidates:
        candidates = [8192]

    initial_rem0 = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    fractions = (0.75, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05)

    for i, nn in enumerate(candidates[:4]):
        rem_now = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem_now < 45.0:
            break
        frac = fractions[i] if i < len(fractions) else 0.05
        alloc = min(float(initial_rem0 * frac), rem_now - 25.0)
        if alloc <= 25.0:
            break
        search_for_n(nn, alloc, rng, parent_arr)

    # Polish whatever is currently best until the internal deadline.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0 and G_BEST_A is not None:
        polish_global(rng, deadline)

    print("before final package: global n=%d obj=%.8f" % (G_BEST_N, G_BEST_OBJ))

    final_list = build_final_list()

    # Final verification. If it fails for any reason, replace with an ultra-safe sequence.
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        ver = float(ver)
        print("final verified length=%d value=%.8f" % (len(final_list), ver))
        if not np.isfinite(ver):
            raise ValueError("evaluate_sequence returned non-finite")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("final verification failed or unavailable, using fallback:", repr(exc))
        a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
        a /= float(a.sum())
        final_list = [float(v) for v in a]
        try:
            ver = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
            print("fallback verified value=%.8f" % ver)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print("returning sequence length=%d" % len(final_list))
    return final_list
```
